#dinar
monetary_system_partiel_dinar = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
			law_dinar_monetary_system_GS_GES_trigger = yes
		}
		localization_key = gold_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			law_dinar_monetary_system_BS_trigger = yes
		}

		localization_key = bimetallism_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			law_dinar_monetary_system_SS_trigger = yes
		}

		localization_key = silver_good
	}
}
